Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the galaxies space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 811 and the convergence rate is O(292/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the galaxies space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 811 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 292. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a galaxies algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 491. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 418/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 491 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the galaxies structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 418. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
